We can exploit pair symmetry. For any pair (i,j) with d>0, contributions to two lags:

Pseudo binary_local_search(h0,n,deadline):
  h = binarize(h0,n)
  S = correlate. M = max
  if M? Track best.
  while time < deadline:

    if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
    T0,T1 = build_matrices(h)
    improved=False
    # Choose order of zeros randomly or by some heuristic? Random to escape.
    order = rng.permutation(zeros)
    for u in order:
       if time > deadline: break
       if h[u]!=0: continue # after previous moves? Since order snapshot, possible u became 1 if swapped? But we update after each move and would need break/rebuild?        A = S + T0[u]
       B = T1[ones]  # view shape m,L? advanced indexing copies, ok
       C = A[None,:] + B
       no_max = C.max(axis=1)
       good = no_max <= M-1
       if not any: continue
       idxg = np.where(good)[0]
       # for good candidates, compute cross
       vgood = ones[idxg]
       d = vgood - u
       p1 = n-1+d; p2 = n-1-d
       cg = C[idxg]
       # newM = max(no_max[idxg], cg[:,p1]+1, cg[:,p2]+1)
       # But if p1==p2 (d=0) not; if p1/p2 same? no.
       # Use maximum method.
       newMs = np.maximum.reduce([no_max[idxg], cg[:,p1]+1, cg[:,p2]+1])
       # choose min newM, tie maybe lower sum? 
       local_best_pos = int(np.argmin(newMs))
       if newMs[local_best_pos] < M:
           v = int(vgood[local_best_pos]); newM = int(newMs[local_best_pos])
           # apply
           h[u]=1; h[v]=0
           d = v-u
           S = S + T0[u] + T1[v]
           S[n-1+d] += 1; S[n-1-d] += 1
           M = newM
           T0,T1 = build_matrices(h)
           ones = np.where(h==1)[0]; zeros = ...
           improved = True
           best update if M*dx < best_c
           # after rebuild, continue to next u? But current u now 1; T0[u] for zero invalid. better restart loop? We can break to new sweep to recompute order. Since improvements may be severe. We can compute loops over l:
grad = np.zeros(n)
for l,p in enumerate(range(-n+1,n)):
  w = weights[p]
  if w == 0 continue
  lo = max(0,-l); hi = min(n-1, n-1-l)
  sources = range lo..hi; targets=sources+l
  grad[sources] -= w * h[targets]
  grad[targets] += w * (1 - h[sources])
This is O(nL)=125k per grad. Python loop 499; each does slice operations lengths average 125. 499* maybe okay if iterations small (few hundred). Gradient implementation optimized:
def smooth_grad(x, alpha):
  n=len(x); c=np.correlate(x,1-x); z=alpha*c; m=z.max(); w=np.exp(z-m); w_sum=w.sum(); w /= w_sum
  # threshold
  nz = np.nonzero(w > 1e-6)[0]
  grad=np.zeros(n)
  h=x
  for p in nz:
    l = p - (n-1)
    wi = w[p]
    if wi <= 0: continue
    if l >=0:
      hi = n-l
      src=slice(0,hi); tgt=slice(l,n)
      grad[src] -= wi*h[tgt]
      grad[tgt] += wi*(1-h[src])
    else:
      d=-l
      src=slice(d,n); tgt=slice(0,n-d)
      # c_l with l negative: i from d to n-1, target i+l = i-d
      grad[src] -= wi*h[tgt]
      grad[tgt] += wi*(1-h[src])
  return grad
Check for l negative: c_l=∑ h_{i+l}(1-h_i), i=d..n-1. Deriv sources i: -h_{i+d}; targets i+d: (1-h_i). So src slice d:n, tgt slice 0:n-d. Good. For l positive: src 0:n-l, tgt l:n. Good.
This gradient is for c counts. Multiplying by dx? If objective C=dx*max c, logsumexp(α c) derivative not scaled by dx. It still minimizes c, same as C. Good.

Smooth objective similarly. Use z=alpha*c; shifted. Return (m + log(sum exp(z-m)))/alpha. This approximates max c. We don't multiply dx. OK.

Could include exact max term? SLSQP objective maybe c_max? nonsmooth. Use logsum.

Projected gradient custom:
def pg_refine(h,n,deadline,seed,alpha=50, iters=2000):
  h=project(h); c0=exact; best
  prev_grad=None; prev_h=None; step=0.05
  for it:
    if time > deadline break
    g = smooth_grad(h,alpha)
    # If grad norm zero break
    # Barzilai-Borwein:
    if prev_grad is not None:
       y = g - prev_g; s = h - prev_h; ys=np.dot(y,s); ss=np.dot(s,s)
       if ys>1e-10 and ss>1e-10: step=ss/ys; clip 1e-3..0.5
       else step=max(1e-4, step*0.5)
    h_trial = project(h - step*g)
    c_trial=exact(h_trial)
    # if no improvement, halve step up to some
    for try in range(10):
       if c_trial <= c0 + 1e-12: update; break
       step*=0.5; h_trial=project(h-step*g); c_trial=exact
    if update:
       if c_trial<best: save
       prev...
    else:
       # try active subgradient? or break
       break
Return best.

 h0=project_feasible(h0,n)
 best_h=h0.copy(); best_c=eval
 if n%2==0:
    hb = binarize(h0,n)
    p1 = t0 + 0.40*(budget_s-10)
    hb,cb = local_search_binary(hb,n,p1,seed=seed, mode='steepest')
    if cb<best_c: update
    # multi-restart binary
    p2 = t0 + 0.70*(budget_s-10)
    for restart in range(...):
       if time>p2: break
       hstart = best_h if binary? Need use current best if binary? If best fractional, binarize best: hbin=binarize(best_h,n).
       perturb: flip rng.randint(5, max(5,n//20)) bits (swap zero/one)
       tl = min(p2-time, 20+rng*20)
       hb,cb = local_search_binary(hpert,n,tl,seed=...)
       if cb<best_c update
       # optional continuous polish this binary: h=cov continuous from hb with 0.02 noise, SLSQP short 10s
       if cb<best_c and time<p2:
           hc=project(hb + 0.02*randn); refine ... time 15
           update
    p3 = t0 + 0.85*(budget_s-10)

    for start in [initial_h, best_h, binarize(best_h)+noise]:

       perturb best binary, local_search 10s
       update
    # final continuous polish with 20s

       update

Projected gradient implementation details:
```
def pg_refine(h,n,deadline,seed,alpha=320, max_steps=500):
    h=project(h); cval=eval; best_h,best_c
    step=0.05
    prev_h=None; prev_g=None
    for it in range(max_steps):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       g=smooth_grad(h,alpha)
       gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gn<1e-12: break
       if prev...
          y=g-prev_g; s=h-prev_h; ys=...; ss=...
          if ys>1e-12: step=ss/ys
       if step<1e-4: step=1e-4; elif step>0.5: step=0.5
       ok=False
       for _ in range(12):
          h2=project(h - step*g)
          c2=eval(h2)
          if c2 <= cval + 1e-13:
             # if c2 < best update
             if c2 < best_c: best...
             prev_h=h; prev_g=g; h=h2; cval=c2; ok=True; break
          step *= 0.5
       if not ok:
          # try smaller random fractional move? or break
          break
       if cval - best_c < 1e-12: maybe step decay
    return best
```

def local_search_binary(h,n,deadline,seed=42, max_sweeps=1000, verbose=False):
    h = binarize(h,n)
    S = np.correlate(h,1-h).astype(np.int32)
    M = int(S.max())
    dx=2.0/n
    best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    L=2*n-1
    sweeps=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       ones=np.where(h==1)[0]; zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]
       if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
       T0,T1=build_T_matrices(h,n)
       improved=False
       # deterministic/random order? Use random permutation of zeros; maybe also consider all.
       order=rng.permutation(zeros)
       for u_pos in order:
           u=int(u_pos)
           if h[u]!=0: continue
           if time.time()>deadline: break
           A=S + T0[u].astype(np.int32)
           B=T1[ones]  # if ones empty
           if B.shape[0]==0: break
           C=A[None,:]+B
           no_max=C.max(axis=1)
           # Since changes integer, need newM < M. no_max may be lower; cross can raise by 1.
           good = no_max <= M-1
           if not np.any(good): continue
           idxg=np.flatnonzero(good)
           vg=ones[idxg]
           d=vg-u
           p1=n-1+d
           p2=n-1-d
           Cg=C[idxg]
           # if p1 or p2 maybe same? d nonzero.
           # Need newM = max(no_max[idxg], Cg[:,p1]+1, Cg[:,p2]+1)
           # Problem: Cg[:,p1]+1 could be less than no_max? max.
           # Use np.maximum with arrays.
           candM = np.maximum(no_max[idxg], Cg[:, p1] + 1)
           candM = np.maximum(candM, Cg[:, p2] + 1)
           # Note: p1/p2 arrays, Cg[:, p1] works fancy indexing along axis? Cg is 2D, p1 1D; Cg[:, p1] in numpy with 1D p1? If Cg shape (k,L) and p1 shape(k,), Cg[:, p1] not allowed? In NumPy, Cg[:, p1] where p1 1D uses advanced indexing on second axis paired with implicit first? Actually `Cg[:, p1]` with p1 1D may error: too many indices? Need use `Cg[np.arange(k), p1]`. Let's do:
           k=idxg.size
           row_idx=np.arange(k)
           val1=Cg[row_idx,p1]+1
           val2=Cg[row_idx,p2]+1
           candM=np.maximum.reduce([no_max[idxg], val1, val2])
           # find min
           pos=int(np.argmin(candM))
           if candM[pos] < M:
               v=int(vg[pos]); newM=int(candM[pos])
               # Apply: recompute S using h? Need update h first, then S = correlate
               h[u]=1; h[v]=0
               S = np.correlate(h,1-h).astype(np.int32)
               M = int(S.max())
               # It should equal newM
               if M < best_c_dx? track integer bestM maybe.
               if M*dx < best_c:
                   best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy(); best_M=M
               improved=True
               # Rebuild T for next iterations
               T0,T1=build_T_matrices(h,n)
               # update ones list? For current loop, ones variable old; advanced B for next u uses ones; recompute to avoid stale
               ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
               # If u was in order, now h[u]=1; next skip.
               # Should we break to new sweep? We can continue. But if many improvements, T rebuild each; okay.
               # However `ones` changed, and `order` includes old zeros; some old ones now zeros (v) not considered as u this sweep.
       if not improved:
           break
       sweeps+=1
    # final recompute
    S=np.correlate(best_h,1-best_h); best_c=float(S.max())*dx
    return best_h,best_c
```

def solve_slsqp(h,n,alpha,maxiter):
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] for n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':con_sum,'jac':con_jac}]
    try: res=minimize(smooth_obj,h,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,jac=smooth_grad,constraints=cons,args=(alpha,),options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-10})
    except: return h
    return res.x if res.success? or res.x could be None.
```

```
def smooth_obj(x, alpha):
    c=np.correlate(x,1-x)
    z=alpha*c
    m=z.max()
    if not np.isfinite(m): return 1e100
    return (m + np.log(np.exp(z-m).sum()))/alpha
```

def continuous_refine(h,n,deadline,seed=42):
    h=project_feasible(h.astype(float).copy(),n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=eval_c5(h,n)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    # SLSQP from current with alphas
    alphas=[20.0,80.0,320.0]
    maxiters=[80,100,80]
    for a,mi in zip(alphas,maxiters):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       x0=current best_h? use current h? Maybe start from best_h each.
       x=solve_slsqp(best_h,n,a,mi)
       if x is not None:
          x=project_feasible(x,n); c=eval
          if c<best_c: update
       # also add small noise and SLSQP
       if time<deadline-30:
          x0=project_feasible(best_h + 0.01*rng.randn(n),n)
          x=solve_slsqp(x0,n,a,mi)
          ...
    # projected gradient from best
    x=pg_refine(best_h,n,deadline-5,seed, alpha=320)
    update
    return best
```

Let's write pseudo code and inspect for bugs.

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5*n
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    if h.size != n: h = h[:n]
    s = h.sum()
    if abs(s-target) < 1e-10:
        return h
    lo = h.min() - 1.0
    hi = h.max() + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        y = np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        if y.sum() > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(h - 0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    # fix residual
    for _ in range(20):
        d = target - h.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-12: break
        if d > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            sroom = room.sum()
            if sroom < 1e-14: break
            h += (d/sroom)*room
        else:
            sh = h.sum()
            if sh < 1e-14: break
            h += (d/sh)*h
        np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    return h
```
The residual adjustment may produce sum slightly off if clip; loop 20. Good.

`eval_c5`:
```
def eval_c5(h,n):
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h)
    return float(c.max()*(2.0/n))
```

`binarize`:
```
def binarize(h,n):
    target = int(round(n/2.0))
    b=np.zeros(n,float)
    if target>0 and target<n:
        idx=np.argpartition(h, n-target)[:target]? For speed. Use argsort small.
        idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1][:target]
        b[idx]=1.0
    return b
```
For n even exact.

`corr_counts`:
```
def corr_counts(h,n):
    return np.rint(np.correlate(h,1.0-h)).astype(np.int32)
```

`build_T`:
```
def build_T(h,n):
    h8=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.int8)
    L=2*n-1
    T0=np.zeros((n,L),dtype=np.int8)
    T1=np.zeros((n,L),dtype=np.int8)
    rev=h8[::-1]
    one_minus_rev=(1 - rev).astype(np.int8)
    neg_h=(-h8).astype(np.int8)
    hm1=(rev - 1).astype(np.int8)
    for p in range(n):
        end=p+n
        T0[p,p:end]=one_minus_rev
        s=n-1-p; e=2*n-1-p
        T0[p,s:e]=neg_h
        T0[p,n-1]=0
        T1[p,p:end]=hm1
        T1[p,s:e]=h8
        T1[p,n-1]=0
    return T0,T1
```
`h8` int8. `1 - rev` with int8? In numpy, 1 - int8 array gives int8? Could overflow? Values 0/1, OK. But to be safe: `(1 - h8[::-1].astype(np.int8)).astype(np.int8)`. `h8[::-1]` view.
`rev - 1` OK.

`local_search_binary`:
Need avoid too many time checks maybe. We'll include `max_sweeps`? Use deadline.
```
def local_search_binary(h, n, deadline, seed=42):
    h = binarize(h, n).copy()
    S = corr_counts(h,n)
    M = int(S.max())
    dx=2.0/n
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=M*dx; best_M=M
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    L=2*n-1
    while time.time() < deadline:
        ones=np.flatnonzero(h>0.5)
        zeros=np.flatnonzero(h<0.5)
        if ones.size==0 or zeros.size==0: break
        T0,T1=build_T(h,n)
        improved=False
        order=rng.permutation(zeros)
        for u_pos in order:
            u=int(u_pos)
            if h[u] >= 0.5: continue
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            A = (S + T0[u]).astype(np.int32, copy=False)
            B = T1[ones]
            C = A[None, :] + B
            no_max = C.max(axis=1)
            good = no_max <= M - 1
            if not good.any(): continue
            idxg = np.flatnonzero(good)
            vg = ones[idxg]
            d = vg - u
            p1 = n - 1 + d
            p2 = n - 1 - d
            Cg = C[idxg]
            k = idxg.size
            ri = np.arange(k)
            vals1 = Cg[ri, p1] + 1
            vals2 = Cg[ri, p2] + 1
            cand = np.maximum(no_max[idxg], vals1)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, vals2)
            pos = int(np.argmin(cand))
            oldM = M
            if cand[pos] < oldM:
                v = int(vg[pos])
                h[u], h[v] = 1.0, 0.0
                S2 = corr_counts(h,n)
                newM = int(S2.max())
                if newM < oldM:
                    S = S2; M = newM
                    if M*dx < best_c:
                        best_c=M*dx; best_M=M; best_h=h.copy()
                    improved=True
                    T0,T1=build_T(h,n)
                    ones=np.flatnonzero(h>0.5)
                    zeros=np.flatnonzero(h<0.5) # not used until next sweep
                    # continue
                else:
                    # revert
                    h[u], h[v] = 0.0, 1.0
                    # S remains old, T matrices old; continue
        if not improved:
            break
    # final exact from best_h
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c
```
Potential bug: `A = (S + T0[u]).astype(np.int32, copy=False)` S int32, T0 row int8. Good.

Now, `perturb_binary`:
```
def perturb_binary(h,n,seed,kmin,kmax):
    b=binarize(h,n).copy()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    k=int(rng.randint(kmin,kmax+1))
    for _ in range(k):
        if b.sum() <=1 or b.sum() >= n-1: break
        # random until i,j diff
        for __ in range(20):
            i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
            if b[i] != b[j]: break
        else: continue
        b[i], b[j] = 1-b[i], 1-b[j]
    return b
```
Since sum n/2 preserved. Good.

`smooth_obj`/`smooth_grad`:
Need if x contains NaN? skip.
```
def smooth_obj(x, alpha):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x)
    z=alpha*c
    m=float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return 1e100
    return (m + float(np.log(np.exp(z-m).sum())))/alpha
```
`smooth_grad`:
```
def smooth_grad(x, alpha):
    n=len(x); c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x)
    z=alpha*c; m=z.max(); w=np.exp(z-m); s=w.sum();
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.zeros(n)
    w /= s
    nz=np.flatnonzero(w > 1e-7)
    g=np.zeros(n)
    h=x
    center=n-1
    for p in nz:
        l=p-center; wi=float(w[p])
        if l>=0:
            end=n-l
            src=np.arange? slices
            g[:end] -= wi*h[l:]
            g[l:] += wi*(1.0-h[:end])
        else:
            d=-l
            g[d:] -= wi*h[:n-d]
            g[:n-d] += wi*(1.0-h[d:])
    return g
```
Check l positive: end=n-l. sources i=0..end-1, targets i+l = l..n-1. g[source] -= wi h[target]; g[target] += wi (1-h[source]). Slices: `g[:end] -= wi*h[l:]`; `g[l:] += wi*(1-h[:end])`. Good.
l negative: d=-l; sources i=d..n-1, targets i-d=0..n-d-1; `g[d:] -= wi*h[:n-d]`; `g[:n-d] += wi*(1-h[d:])`. Good.
Threshold 1e-7; for α small all weights included; loop 499. Fine.
Could normalize by 1/alpha? We decided derivative = sum w ∂c. Yes.

`con_sum`:
```
def con_sum(x): return x.sum() - 0.5*len(x)
def con_jac(x): return np.ones(len(x))
```

`slsqp_polish`:
```
def slsqp_polish(x, n, alpha, maxiter):
    x = project_feasible(np.asarray(x,dtype=float), n)
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons = [{'type':'eq','fun':con_sum,'jac':con_jac}]
    try:
       res = minimize(smooth_obj, x, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds,
                      jac=smooth_grad, constraints=cons, args=(alpha,),
                      options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-11})
    except Exception:
       return x
    if res is None: return x
    xm = getattr(res,'x',None)
    if xm is None: return x
    return project_feasible(xm,n)
```
Need import minimize at top. If SLSQP maxiter 0? ensure int >0.

SLSQP may be very slow with bounds list and constraints every call; okay.

`pg_refine`:

```
def pg_refine(h,n,deadline,seed,alpha=200.0,max_iter=10000):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=eval_c5(h,n)
    cur_c=best_c
    step=0.05
    prev_h=None; prev_g=None
    it_no_improve=0
    while time.time()<deadline:
        g=smooth_grad(h,alpha)
        gn=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gn < 1e-12: break
        if prev_h is not None:
            s=h-prev_h; y=g-prev_g; ys=float(np.dot(y,s)); ss=float(np.dot(s,s))
            if ys>1e-12 and ss>1e-12:
                step=ss/ys
            if not np.isfinite(step) or step<=0: step=max(1e-4, step*0.5 if step>0 else 0.05)
        if step<1e-5: step=1e-5
        if step>0.25: step=0.25
        accepted=False
        cur_step=step
        for _ in range(15):
            h2=project_feasible(h - cur_step*g, n)
            c2=eval_c5(h2,n)
            if c2 <= cur_c + 1e-12:
                h=h2; cur_c=c2
                if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
                    best_c=c2; best_h=h.copy()
                accepted=True
                break
            cur_step *= 0.5
        if not accepted:
            # try a few random small fractional moves
            # or break
            break
        prev_h=h.copy(); prev_g=g
        if cur_c > best_c - 1e-13:
            # at best maybe still step
            pass
        it_no_improve += 0
    return best_h,best_c
```
But if current h not best (SLSQP may worsen), pg starts from h. We call with best_h. If no descent, break.
The line search may accept equal cur_c due to numerical. Good.
Could get stuck because project_feasible bisection changes h in ways. Step maybe too large; line search halves. If no accept, maybe try random perturbation within time. We can add:
```
if not accepted:
   for _ in range(20):
      h2=project_feasible(h + 0.01*rng.randn(n),n); c2=eval; if c2<cur_c: accept break
   if not: break
```

`continuous_refine`:
```
def continuous_refine(h,n,deadline,seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h=project_feasible(np.asarray(h,float),n); best_c=eval_c5(best_h,n)
    cur_h=best_h.copy()
    alphas=[30.0, 100.0, 400.0]
    iters=[50, 80, 80]
    for a,mi in zip(alphas,iters):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        x=slsqp_polish(cur_h,n,a,mi)
        c=eval_c5(x,n)
        if c < best_c - 1e-13:
            best_c=c; best_h=x.copy()
        cur_h=best_h.copy()
        if time.time() > deadline - 30: break
        # small random restart
        r=0.02 + 0.03*rng.random()
        x=slsqp_polish(project_feasible(best_h + r*rng.randn(n),n),n,a,mi)
        c=eval_c5(x,n)
        if c < best_c - 1e-13:
            best_c=c; best_h=x.copy()
    # PGD with high alpha
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
        x,c=pg_refine(best_h,n,deadline-5,seed,alpha=400.0)
        if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=x.copy()
    # final small SLSQP
    if time.time() < deadline - 2:
        x=slsqp_polish(best_h,n,800.0,40)
        c=eval_c5(x,n)
        if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=x.copy()
    return project_feasible(best_h,n), float(eval_c5(project_feasible(best_h,n),n))
```
But SLSQP calls can exceed deadline unexpectedly. We check before; if one takes 100s, final may late. But run deadline parent manages.

Maybe using SLSQP with maxiter too many. Set maxiter proportional: 30-50. For n=250, 50 okay. Let's use alphas [20,80,300], maxiters [40,60,60]. plus final 30.

What if SLSQP `fail` returns x worse; update only if better. `cur_h` remains best. Good.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):

    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()

    except Exception:
       n=250

    h0=project_feasible(h0,n)

    # if parent value maybe >? but ok.
    if n % 2 == 0:
       # local search from binarized parent
       p1=t0 + 0.35*max(10,budget_s-10)
       hb,cb=local_search_binary(h0,n,p1,seed=seed)

       # more restarts until p2

       # continuous refine main

          update

    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_c=eval_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c,n

def penalty_obj(x, alpha, mu, target):
    return smooth_obj(x,alpha) + mu*(x.sum()-target)**2

def lbfgsb_polish(x,n,alpha,mu,maxiter):

Pseudo final run:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    total=max(10.0, float(budget_s)-8.0)
    deadline=t0+total
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       n=len(h0)
    except Exception:
       n=250
       h0=np.full(n,0.5)
    if n<=0: n=250; h0=np.full(n,0.5)
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n)
    best_h=h0.copy()
    best_c=eval_c5(h0,n)
    # quick continuous from parent
    if time.time() < deadline - 20:
       lim=min(deadline-20, t0+0.08*total)
       if lim > time.time()+10:
          hc,cc=continuous_refine(h0,n,lim,seed+1)
          if cc < best_c: best_c=cc; best_h=hc.copy()
    if n % 2 == 0:
       # binary local initial
       lim=min(deadline-15, t0+0.35*total)
       if time.time() < lim-5:
          hb,cb=local_search_binary(h0,n,lim,seed+2)
          if cb<best_c: best_c=cb; best_h=hb.copy()
       # restarts
       lim=min(deadline-15, t0+0.65*total)
       restart=0
       while time.time() < lim-10 and restart < 300:
           restart +=1
           if best_c <= 0.3805: # good enough? still maybe continue binary? Could break to continuous
               break
           kmin=int(3+rng.random()*5)
           kmax=int(max(kmin+2, 20+rng.random()*40))
           hp=perturb_binary(best_h,n,seed+100+restart*17,kmin,kmax)
           rem=lim-time.time()-8
           if rem < 4: break
           t_sub=min(20.0 + 40.0*rng.random(), rem)
           hb,cb=local_search_binary(hp,n,time.time()+t_sub,seed+200+restart*31)
           if cb<best_c:
               best_c=cb; best_h=hb.copy()
           # quick polish if new binary
           if cb < 0.384 and time.time() < lim-40:
               hc,cc=continuous_refine(hb,n,time.time()+25,seed+300+restart)
               if cc<best_c:
                   best_c=cc; best_h=hc.copy()
       # main continuous
       lim=min(deadline-10, t0+0.85*total)
       if time.time() < lim-15:
           # from current best
           hc,cc=continuous_refine(best_h,n,min(lim, time.time()+120),seed+400)
           if cc<best_c: update
           if time.time() < lim-40:
               # from original parent
               hc,cc=continuous_refine(h0,n,min(lim, time.time()+80),seed+401)
               if cc<best_c: update
       # final binary
       lim=min(deadline-8, t0+0.93*total)
       while time.time() < lim-8 and best_c > 0.3808:
           restart +=1
           hp=perturb_binary(best_h,n,seed+500+restart*23,3,18)
           rem=lim-time.time()-4
           if rem<3: break
           hb,cb=local_search_binary(hp,n,time.time()+min(15.0,rem),seed+600+restart*41)
           if cb<best_c:
               best_c=cb; best_h=hb.copy()
    else:
       # odd n continuous only
       if time.time() < deadline-10:
          hc,cc=continuous_refine(h0,n,deadline-10,seed)
          update
    # final continuous
    if time.time() < deadline-2:
       hc,cc=continuous_refine(best_h,n,deadline-2,seed+777)
       if cc<best_c: update
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_c=eval_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c,n
```

def continuous_refine(h,n,deadline,seed):
    best_h=project_feasible(...)
    best_c=eval
    rng=...
    # L-BFGS
    for a in (20.0,80.0,300.0):

       update

    # final small SLSQP

Let's write pg:

def pg_refine(h,n,deadline,seed,alpha=400.0):
    h=project; best
    cur_c=best
    step=0.05
    prev_h=None; prev_g=None
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    while time.time()<deadline:
       g=smooth_grad(h,alpha)
       gn=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
       if gn<1e-10: break
       if step is None or not finite: step=0.1/max(1.0,gn)
       if prev...
       step=clip(step,1e-5,0.2)
       accepted=False; s=step
       for _ in range(18):
          h2=project_feasible(h - s*g,n)
          c2=eval_c5(h2,n)
          if c2 <= cur_c + 1e-12:
             ...; break
          s*=0.5
       if not accepted:
          # try random perturb few
          ok=False
          for _ in range(10):
             scale=0.02 + 0.03*rng.random()
             h2=project_feasible(h + scale*rng.randn(n),n)
             c2=eval
             if c2 < cur_c - 1e-12: accept; ok=True; break
             elif c2 < best_c+1e-12: maybe set current? no
          if not ok: break
       prev_h=h; prev_g=g
    return best
```

def binary_energy_search(h,n,deadline,seed,max_moves=5000):
    h=binarize(h,n).copy()
    S=corr_counts(h,n); M=int(S.max()); energy=int(np.sum(S.astype(np.int64)**2))
    dx=2/n; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=M*dx; best_M=M
    rng=...
    moves=0
    while time.time()<deadline and moves<max_moves:
       ones=zeros...
       if none break
       T0,T1=build_T(h,n)
       # choose a random zero maybe rather than all to save
       u=int(rng.choice(zeros))
       A=(S+T0[u]).astype(np.int32)
       B=T1[ones]; C=A[None,:]+B
       oldM=M
       no_max=C.max(axis=1); no_sum=(C*C).sum(axis=1).astype(np.int64)
       goodM = no_max <= oldM  # because cross may raise
       idxg=flatnonzero(goodM)
       if idxg.size==0: continue
       vg=ones[idxg]; d=vg-u; p1=...; Cg=C[idxg]
       vals1=Cg[ri,p1]+1; vals2=...
       candM=np.maximum.reduce([no_max[idxg],vals1,vals2]).astype(np.int64)
       cross_delta = (vals1*2 - Cg[ri,p1])? Since (x+1)^2-x^2 = 2x+1. Use C before +1.
       e = no_sum[idxg] + (2*Cg[ri,p1]+1) + (2*Cg[ri,p2]+1)
       accept = (candM < oldM) | (candM == oldM & (e < energy))
       if any:
          pos=argmin where accept maybe prefer lowest candM then energy
          apply; recompute S,M,energy; if M<best_M update best
          T0,T1=build? We can rebuild each move; moves limited.
          moves+=1
       else:
          # maybe try next random u; limit attempts
          attempts...
    return best_h,best_c
```

Maybe the previous `slsqp_smooth` objective had a weird formula: `return m + (sm + np.log(np.exp(sh - sm).sum())) / alpha`? Wait previous code:
```
sh = alpha * (g - m)
sm = sh.max()
return m + (sm + np.log(np.exp(sh - sm).sum())) / alpha
```
Here m = g.max(). Equivalent to max + logsumexp(alpha(g-m))/alpha = max + max(g-m)+.../alpha? Since g-m≤0, sm≤0. This objective equals m + (sm + logsum)/alpha. Since sm = alpha*(g-m).max() ≤0. This approximates max but has an extra term ≤0? Actually logsumexp(alpha(g-m))/alpha = max(g-m) + logsum(exp(...))/alpha. So m + that = m + max(g-m) + ... = max g + small. OK. ```
def local_search_binary(h,n,deadline,seed=42):
    h=binarize(h,n).copy(); S=corr_counts(h,n); M=int(S.max()); dx=2.0/n
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=M*dx; rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    while time.time()<deadline:
        ones=np.flatnonzero(h>0.5); zeros=np.flatnonzero(h<0.5)
        if not ones.size or not zeros.size: break
        T0,T1=build_T(h,n); improved=False
        for u in rng.permutation(zeros):
            u=int(u)
            if h[u]>=0.5 or time.time()>deadline: continue
            A=(S+T0[u]).astype(np.int32)
            B=T1[ones]; C=A[None,:]+B; nm=C.max(axis=1)
            g=nm<=M-1
            if not g.any(): continue
            ig=np.flatnonzero(g); vg=ones[ig]; d=vg-u; p1=n-1+d; p2=n-1-d
            Cg=C[ig]; k=len(ig); ri=np.arange(k)
            cm=np.maximum(nm[ig], Cg[ri,p1]+1); cm=np.maximum(cm, Cg[ri,p2]+1)
            pos=int(np.argmin(cm))
            oldM=M
            if cm[pos]<oldM:
                v=int(vg[pos]); h[u],h[v]=1.0,0.0
                S=corr_counts(h,n); newM=int(S.max())
                if newM<oldM:
                    M=newM
                    if M*dx<best_c: best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy()
                    improved=True; T0,T1=build_T(h,n); ones=np.flatnonzero(h>0.5)
                else: h[u],h[v]=0.0,1.0; S=corr_counts(h,n); M=oldM
        if not improved: break
    best_c=eval_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c
```

Penalty functions:
```
def pen_obj(x, alpha, mu, target): return smooth_obj(x,alpha)+mu*(x.sum()-target)**2
def pen_grad(x, alpha, mu, target): return smooth_grad(x,alpha)+2*mu*(x.sum()-target)
def lbfgsb(x,n,alpha,maxiter):
   x=project(x,n); bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   try: res=minimize(pen_obj,x,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,jac=pen_grad,args=(alpha,1e4,0.5*n),options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
   except: return x
   return project(res.x,n)
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); total=max(10.0,float(budget_s)-8); deadline=t0+total
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float); n=len(h0)
    except Exception:
       n=250; h0=np.full(n,0.5)
    if n<=0: n=250; h0=np.full(n,0.5)
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n)
    best_h=h0.copy(); best_c=eval_c5(h0,n)
    bin_h=None; bin_c=None
    if n%2==0:
       bin_h=binarize(h0,n); bin_c=eval_c5(bin_h,n)
       if total>180:
          lim=min(deadline-30,t0+0.07*total)
          if lim>time.time()+15:
             hh,cc=continuous_refine(h0,n,lim,seed+1); update
       lim=min(deadline-20,t0+0.35*total)
       if time.time()<lim-5:
          bh,bc=local_search_binary(bin_h,n,lim,seed+2)
          if bc<bin_c: bin_h,bin_c=bh,bc
          if bc<best_c: best_h,best_c=bh,bc
       # restarts
       lim2=min(deadline-20,t0+0.68*total); r=0
       while time.time()<lim2-10 and bin_c is not None and r<400:
           r+=1
           kmin=3+int(5*rng.random()); kmax=kmin+15+int(35*rng.random())
           hp=perturb_binary(bin_h,n,seed+100+r,kmin,kmax)
           tsub=min(20+40*rng.random(), lim2-time.time()-8)
           if tsub<3: break
          bh,bc=local_search_binary(hp,n,time.time()+tsub,seed+200+r)
          if bc<bin_c: bin_h,bin_c=bh,bc
          if bc<best_c: best_h,best_c=bh,bc
       # continuous from parent/best
       lim3=min(deadline-10,t0+0.88*total)
       if time.time()<lim3-30:
          hh,cc=continuous_refine(best_h,n,min(lim3,time.time()+120),seed+300); update
          if bin_h is not None and time.time()<lim3-30:
             hh,cc=continuous_refine(bin_h,n,min(lim3,time.time()+80),seed+301); update
       # final binary if above target
       lim4=min(deadline-8,t0+0.95*total)
       while time.time()<lim4-5 and (bin_c is None or bin_c>0.376) and best_c>0.3808:
          r+=1; hp=perturb_binary(bin_h if bin_h is not None else best_h,n,seed+400+r,3,15)
          tsub=min(12.0, lim4-time.time()-4)
          if tsub<2: break
          bh,bc=local_search_binary(hp,n,time.time()+tsub,seed+500+r)
          if bin_h is not None:
             if bc<bin_c: bin_h,bin_c=bh,bc
          if bc<best_c: best_h,best_c=bh,bc
       if time.time()<deadline-4:
          hh,cc=continuous_refine(best_h,n,deadline-3,seed+600); update
    else:
       hh,cc=continuous_refine(h0,n,deadline-5,seed); update
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n); best_c=eval_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c,n

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def eval_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h)) * (2.0 / n))

def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy().reshape(-1)
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.resize(h, n)
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    if abs(h.sum() - target) < 1e-11:
        return h
    lo, hi = h.min() - 1.0, h.max() + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        y = np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        if y.sum() > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(h - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(12):
        d = target - h.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-12:
            break
        if d > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            s = room.sum()
            if s < 1e-14:
                break
            h = h + (d / s) * room
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-14:
                break
            h = h + (d / s) * h
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h

def binarize(h, n):
    target = int(round(0.5 * n))
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if 0 < target < n:
        b[np.argsort(h)[::-1][:target]] = 1.0
    return b

def corr_counts(h, n):
    return np.rint(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h)).astype(np.int32)

def build_T(h, n):
    h8 = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.int8)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    T0 = np.zeros((n, L), dtype=np.int8)
    T1 = np.zeros((n, L), dtype=np.int8)
    rev = h8[::-1]
    t0val = (1 - rev).astype(np.int8)
    s0val = (-rev[0::-1]).astype(np.int8) if n > 0 else rev.astype(np.int8)
    s0val = (-h8).astype(np.int8)
    t1val = (rev - 1).astype(np.int8)
    s1val = h8.astype(np.int8)
    for p in range(n):
        e = p + n
        T0[p, p:e] += t0val
        s = n - 1 - p
        T0[p, s:s + n] += s0val
        T0[p, n - 1] = 0
        T1[p, p:e] += t1val
        T1[p, s:s + n] += s1val
        T1[p, n - 1] = 0
    return T0, T1

def local_search_binary(h, n, deadline, seed=42):
    h = binarize(h, n).copy()
    S = corr_counts(h, n)
    M = int(S.max())
    dx = 2.0 / n
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = M * dx
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    while time.time() < deadline:
        ones = np.flatnonzero(h > 0.5)
        zeros = np.flatnonzero(h < 0.5)
        if ones.size == 0 or zeros.size == 0:
            break
        T0, T1 = build_T(h, n)
        improved = False
        for u_pos in rng.permutation(zeros):
            u = int(u_pos)
            if h[u] >= 0.5 or time.time() > deadline:
                continue
            A = (S + T0[u]).astype(np.int32)
            B = T1[ones]
            C = A[None, :] + B
            nm = C.max(axis=1)
            g = nm <= M - 1
            if not g.any():
                continue
            ig = np.flatnonzero(g)
            vg = ones[ig]
            d = vg - u
            p1 = n - 1 + d
            p2 = n - 1 - d
            Cg = C[ig]
            k = ig.size
            ri = np.arange(k)
            cm = np.maximum(nm[ig], Cg[ri, p1] + 1)
            cm = np.maximum(cm, Cg[ri, p2] + 1)
            pos = int(np.argmin(cm))
            oldM = M
            if cm[pos] < oldM:
                v = int(vg[pos])
                h[u], h[v] = 1.0, 0.0
                S = corr_counts(h, n)
                newM = int(S.max())
                if newM < oldM:
                    M = newM
                    if M * dx < best_c:
                        best_c = M * dx
                        best_h = h.copy()
                    improved = True
                    T0, T1 = build_T(h, n)
                    ones = np.flatnonzero(h > 0.5)
                else:
                    h[u], h[v] = 0.0, 1.0
                    S = corr_counts(h, n)
                    M = oldM
        if not improved:
            break
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c

def perturb_binary(h, n, seed, kmin, kmax):
    b = binarize(h, n).copy()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    kmin = max(1, int(kmin))
    kmax = max(kmin, int(kmax))
    k = int(rng.randint(kmin, kmax + 1))
    for _ in range(k):
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        for __ in range(25):
            j = int(rng.randint(n))
            if b[i] != b[j]:
                break
        else:
            continue
        b[i], b[j] = 1.0 - b[i], 1.0 - b[j]
    return b

def smooth_obj(x, alpha):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x)
    m = c.max()
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return 1e100
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    return m + (float(np.log(np.exp(z - z.max()).sum())) + float(z.max())) / alpha

def smooth_grad(x, alpha):
    n = len(x)
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x)
    z = alpha * c
    m = z.max()
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.zeros(n)
    w = np.exp(z - m)
    s = w.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros(n)
    w /= s
    nz = np.flatnonzero(w > 1e-8)
    g = np.zeros(n)
    h = x
    center = n - 1
    for p in nz:
        l = p - center
        wi = float(w[p])
        if l >= 0:
            end = n - l
            g[:end] -= wi * h[l:]
            g[l:] += wi * (1.0 - h[:end])
        else:
            d = -l
            g[d:] -= wi * h[:n - d]
            g[:n - d] += wi * (1.0 - h[d:])
    return g

def con_sum(x):
    return x.sum() - 0.5 * len(x)

def con_jac(x):
    return np.ones(len(x))

def slsqp_polish(x, n, alpha, maxiter):
    x = project_feasible(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), n)
    if maxiter < 1:
        return x
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': con_sum, 'jac': con_jac}]
    try:
        res = minimize(smooth_obj, x, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds,
                       jac=smooth_grad, constraints=cons, args=(float(alpha),),
                       options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-11})
    except Exception:
        return x
    y = getattr(res, 'x', None)
    return project_feasible(y, n) if y is not None else x

def pen_obj(x, alpha, mu, target):
    return smooth_obj(x, alpha) + mu * (x.sum() - target) ** 2

def pen_grad(x, alpha, mu, target):
    return smooth_grad(x, alpha) + (2.0 * mu * (x.sum() - target)) * np.ones_like(x)

def lbfgsb_polish(x, n, alpha, maxiter):
    x = project_feasible(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), n)
    if maxiter < 1:
        return x
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    target = 0.5 * n
    try:
        res = minimize(pen_obj, x, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                       jac=pen_grad, args=(float(alpha), 1e4, target),
                       options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-12})
    except Exception:
        return x
    y = getattr(res, 'x', None)
    return project_feasible(y, n) if y is not None else x

def continuous_refine(h, n, deadline, seed):
    best = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), n)
    bc = eval_c5(best, n)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    for a in (20.0, 80.0, 250.0):
        if time.time() > deadline - 18:
            break
        x = lbfgsb_polish(best, n, a, 55)
        c = eval_c5(x, n)
        if c < bc:
            bc, best = c, x.copy()
        if time.time() > deadline - 12:
            continue
        sc = 0.012 + 0.035 * rng.random()
        x = lbfgsb_polish(project_feasible(best + sc * rng.randn(n), n), n, a, 40)
        c = eval_c5(x, n)
        if c < bc:
            bc, best = c, x.copy()
    for a, mi in ((40.0, 55), (150.0, 75)):
        if time.time() > deadline - 35:
            break
        x = slsqp_polish(best, n, a, mi)
        c = eval_c5(x, n)
        if c < bc:
            bc, best = c, x.copy()
        if time.time() > deadline - 28:
            continue
        sc = 0.010 + 0.030 * rng.random()
        x = slsqp_polish(project_feasible(best + sc * rng.randn(n), n), n, a, mi)
        c = eval_c5(x, n)
        if c < bc:
            bc, best = c, x.copy()
    if time.time() < deadline - 3:
        x = slsqp_polish(best, n, 700.0, 35)
        c = eval_c5(x, n)
        if c < bc:
            bc, best = c, x.copy()
    best = project_feasible(best, n)
    return best, eval_c5(best, n)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0)
    deadline = t0 + total
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = len(h0)
    except Exception:
        n = 250
        h0 = np.full(n, 0.5)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 250
        h0 = np.full(n, 0.5)
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = eval_c5(h0, n)
    bin_h = None
    bin_c = None
    if n % 2 == 0:
        bin_h = binarize(h0, n)
        bin_c = eval_c5(bin_h, n)
        if total > 150:
            lim = min(deadline - 35.0, t0 + 0.07 * total)
            if lim > time.time() + 15.0:
                hh, cc = continuous_refine(h0, n, lim, int(seed) + 1)
                if cc < best_c:
                    best_c, best_h = cc, hh.copy()
        lim = min(deadline - 25.0, t0 + 0.36 * total)
        if time.time() < lim - 5.0:
            bh, bc = local_search_binary(bin_h, n, lim, int(seed) + 2)
            if bc < bin_c:
                bin_c, bin_h = bc, bh.copy()
            if bc < best_c:
                best_c, best_h = bc, bh.copy()
        lim2 = min(deadline - 25.0, t0 + 0.68 * total)
        r = 0
        while time.time() < lim2 - 10.0 and bin_h is not None:
            r += 1
            kmin = 3 + int(5 * rng.random())
            kmax = kmin + 15 + int(35 * rng.random())
            hp = perturb_binary(bin_h, n, int(seed) + 100 + r, kmin, kmax)
            tsub = min(20.0 + 45.0 * rng.random(), lim2 - time.time() - 8.0)
            if tsub < 3.0:
                break
            bh, bc = local_search_binary(hp, n, time.time() + tsub, int(seed) + 200 + r)
            if bc < bin_c:
                bin_c, bin_h = bc, bh.copy()
            if bc < best_c:
                best_c, best_h = bc, bh.copy()
        lim3 = min(deadline - 12.0, t0 + 0.88 * total)
        if time.time() < lim3 - 30.0:
            hh, cc = continuous_refine(best_h, n, min(lim3, time.time() + 130.0), int(seed) + 300)
            if cc < best_c:
                best_c, best_h = cc, hh.copy()
            if bin_h is not None and bin_c < 0.385 and time.time() < lim3 - 30.0:
                hh, cc = continuous_refine(bin_h, n, min(lim3, time.time() + 80.0), int(seed) + 301)
                if cc < best_c:
                    best_c, best_h = cc, hh.copy()
        lim4 = min(deadline - 8.0, t0 + 0.94 * total)
        while time.time() < lim4 - 5.0 and best_c > 0.3808 and bin_h is not None:
            r += 1
            hp = perturb_binary(bin_h, n, int(seed) + 400 + r, 3, 18)
            tsub = min(14.0, lim4 - time.time() - 4.0)
            if tsub < 2.0:
                break
            bh, bc = local_search_binary(hp, n, time.time() + tsub, int(seed) + 500 + r)
            if bc < bin_c:
                bin_c, bin_h = bc, bh.copy()
            if bc < best_c:
                best_c, best_h = bc, bh.copy()
        if time.time() < deadline - 4.0:
            hh, cc = continuous_refine(best_h, n, deadline - 3.0, int(seed) + 600)
            if cc < best_c:
                best_c, best_h = cc, hh.copy()
    else:
        if time.time() < deadline - 8.0:
            hh, cc = continuous_refine(h0, n, deadline - 5.0, int(seed))
            if cc < best_c:
                best_c, best_h = cc, hh.copy()
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c, n
```
